547        1/1              assign cur_patch = patch_base_reg + {10'd0, out_row};
548                         logic [ 9:0] blk;
549        1/1              assign blk       = cur_patch[11:2];                                   // 2x2 block index
550                         logic [ 1:0] q;
551        1/1              assign q         = cur_patch[1:0];                                    // (x,y): 0=(0,0) 1=(1,0) 2=(0,1) 3=(1,1)
552                         logic [ 2:0] wpr_sh;
553        1/1              assign wpr_sh    = (conv_w_sh_reg == 3'd0) ? 3'd0 : (conv_w_sh_reg - 3'd1);   // log2(W/2)

556                         logic [ 9:0] bx;

568                             current_position  // [11:0] position

                                                                     ==>  
                                                                     ==>  
565                            end else if (!row_mask[patch_lane]) begin
                                        -7-  
566                                patch_lane <= patch_lane + 2'd1;
                                   ==>
567                            end else if (cur_hit) begin
                                        -8-  
568                                patch_lane <= patch_lane + 2'd1;
                                   ==>
569                            end else begin
570                                win_valid[cur_bank] <= 1'b0;
                                   ==>
571                                state <= S_CAP_INIT;
572                            end
573                        end
574        
575                        S_CAP_INIT: begin
576                            cap_x   <= org_x;
                               ==>
577                            cap_y   <= org_y;
578                            cap_lin <= org_L;
579                            cap_cx  <= 2'd0;
580                            cap_cy  <= 2'd0;
581                            cap_grp <= 1'b0;
582                            state   <= S_CAP_REQ;
583                        end
584        
585                        // out-of-range (padding) pixels stored as 0
586                        S_CAP_REQ: begin
587                            if (cap_in_range) begin
                               -9-  
588                                o_rd_en   <= 1'b1;
                                   ==>
589                                o_rd_addr <= cap_addr_full[13:0];
590                                state     <= S_CAP_WAIT;
591                            end else begin
592                                window_mem[{cur_bank, cap_word}] <= 24'd0;
                                   ==>
593                                cap_advance;
594                            end
595                        end
596        
597                        S_CAP_WAIT: begin
598                            if (i_rd_valid) begin
                               -10-  
599                                window_mem[{cur_bank, cap_word}] <= i_rd_data;
                                   ==>
600                                cap_advance;
601                            end
                               MISSING_ELSE
                               ==>
602                        end
603        
604                        S_SEND: begin
605                            if (o_valid && i_ready) begin
                               -11-  
606                                if (send_k == k_total - 13'd1) begin
                                   -12-  
607                                    state <= S_IDLE;
                                       ==>
608                                end else begin
609                                    send_k <= send_k + 13'd1;
610                                    if (send_ch == in_c - 6'd1) begin
                                       -13-  
611                                        send_byte <= 2'd0;
612                                        send_grp  <= 1'b0;
613                                        if (send_kx == 2'd2) begin
                                           -14-  
614                                            send_kx <= 2'd0;
                                               ==>
615                                            send_ky <= send_ky + 2'd1;
616                                        end else begin
617                                            send_kx <= send_kx + 2'd1;
                                               ==>
618                                        end
619                                    end else begin
620                                        if (send_byte == 2'd2) begin
                                           -15-  
621                                            send_byte <= 2'd0;
                                               ==>
622                                            send_grp  <= 1'b1;
623                                        end else begin
624                                            send_byte <= send_byte + 2'd1;
                                               ==>
625                                        end
626                                    end
627                                end
628                            end
                               MISSING_ELSE
                               ==>
629                        end
630        
631                        default: state <= S_IDLE;
                           ==>

382                             ly_k
383                         );
384                     
385                         // next chunk within a tile
386                         logic [`K_W-1:0]     nx_kbase;
387        1/1              assign nx_kbase = svc_kbase + {{(`K_W-`CHUNK_W){1'b0}}, svc_len};
388                         logic [`CHUNK_W-1:0] nx_len;
389        1/1              assign nx_len = chunk_len(ly_k - nx_kbase);
390                     
391                         logic chunk_accept;
392        1/1              assign chunk_accept = (state == C_PE_WAIT) && (wsvc == W_IDLE) && i_chunk_req;
393                     
394                         // mem_base = W_BASE + oc_group*K + k_base; max 45,249 fits in 16 bits.
395                         logic [`W_AW-1:0] og_ext;
396        1/1              assign og_ext = {{(`W_AW - `OCG_W) {1'b0}}, oc_group};
397                         logic [`W_AW-1:0] k_ext;
398        1/1              assign k_ext = {{(`W_AW - `K_W) {1'b0}}, ly_k};
399                         logic [`W_AW-1:0] kb_ext;
400        1/1              assign kb_ext = {{(`W_AW - `K_W) {1'b0}}, svc_kbase};
401                         logic [`W_AW-1:0] og_off;
402        1/1              assign og_off = og_off_q;
403        1/1              assign o_mem_base         = ly_w_base + og_off + kb_ext;
404                     
405                         // W_ISSUE waits for ready; start pulses on that clk.
406        1/1              assign o_wload_start      = (wsvc == W_ISSUE) && i_wload_ready;
407        1/1              assign o_chunk_word_count = svc_len;
408        1/1              assign o_chunk_loaded     = (wsvc == W_REPLY);  // W_REPLY lasts 1clk
409        1/1              assign o_buf_half         = buf_half;
410                     
411                         // ---- Param RAM -> param_buf bulk load (once per inference) ----
412                         // Records packed in layer order. Fixed 1clk read latency, 2clk per record.
413                         localparam [`PARAM_AW:0] PARAM_TOTAL =
414                               (NUM_LAYERS <= 1) ? (L0_PARAM_BASE + L0_OUT_C) :
415                               (NUM_LAYERS == 2) ? (L1_PARAM_BASE + L1_OUT_C) :
416                               (NUM_LAYERS == 3) ? (L2_PARAM_BASE + L2_OUT_C) :
417                                                   (L3_PARAM_BASE + L3_OUT_C);
418                     
419                         logic [`PARAM_AW:0] pl_idx;  // 0 .. PARAM_TOTAL (1bit headroom)
420                         logic pl_busy;
421                         logic pl_done;
422                         logic [`PARAM_AW-1:0] pl_addr_q;
423                     
424                         logic pl_more;
425        1/1              assign pl_more = (pl_idx < PARAM_TOTAL);
426                     
427        1/1              assign o_ram_owner = (state == C_PARAM_LOAD);
428                     
429        1/1              assign o_param_mem_addr = pl_idx[`PARAM_AW-1:0];
430                     
431                         logic pmem_req_fire;
432        1/1              assign pmem_req_fire = (state == C_PARAM_LOAD) && pl_more && !pl_busy;   // read issued
433                         logic pmem_rsp_fire;
434        1/1              assign pmem_rsp_fire = pl_busy;                                            // data from previous clk's address
435                     
436        1/1              assign o_param_wr_en = pmem_rsp_fire;
437        1/1              assign o_param_wr_addr = pl_addr_q;
438        1/1              assign o_param_wr_data = i_param_mem_data;
439                     
440        1/1              assign o_tile_valid = (state == C_PE_TILE) && tile_started;
441                     
442                         // 1clk on first i_tile_ready in C_TILE_CFG; tcfg_sent prevents a repeat.
443        1/1              assign o_tile_cfg_valid = (state == C_TILE_CFG) && !tcfg_sent && i_tile_ready;
444                     
445                         always_ff @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
446        1/1                  if (!rst_n) begin
447        1/1                      state <= C_IDLE;
448        1/1                      layer_idx <= 3'd0;
449        1/1                      region_sel <= 1'b0;
450        1/1                      pos_group         <= {`POS_W{1'b0}};
451        1/1                      oc_group          <= {`OCG_W{1'b0}};
452        1/1                      og_off_q          <= {`W_AW{1'b0}};
453        1/1                      tile_started <= 1'b0;
454        1/1                      tcfg_sent <= 1'b0;
455        1/1                      first_chunk_rdy <= 1'b0;
456        1/1                      tile_in_done_seen <= 1'b0;
457        1/1                      layer_done_seen <= 1'b0;
458        1/1                      o_ld_start <= 1'b0;
459        1/1                      o_param_load_start <= 1'b0;
460        1/1                      ld_done_seen <= 1'b0;
461        1/1                      o_write_grant <= 1'b0;
462        1/1                      o_layer_cfg_valid <= 1'b0;
463        1/1                      o_pg_tile_start <= 1'b0;
464        1/1                      o_fc_tile_start <= 1'b0;
465        1/1                      wsvc <= W_IDLE;
466        1/1                      svc_kbase         <= {`K_W{1'b0}};
467        1/1                      svc_len           <= {`CHUNK_W{1'b0}};
468        1/1                      buf_half          <= 1'b0;
469        2/2                      wtag_valid[0] <= 1'b0;  wtag_valid[1] <= 1'b0;
470        2/2                      wtag_layer[0] <= 3'd0;  wtag_layer[1] <= 3'd0;
471        1/1                      wtag_oc[0]        <= {`OCG_W{1'b0}};
472        1/1                      wtag_oc[1]        <= {`OCG_W{1'b0}};
473        1/1                      pl_idx            <= {(`PARAM_AW+1){1'b0}};
474        1/1                      pl_busy <= 1'b0;
475        1/1                      pl_done <= 1'b0;
476        1/1                      pl_addr_q         <= {`PARAM_AW{1'b0}};
477                             end else begin
478        1/1                      o_ld_start    <= 1'b0;
479        1/1                      o_param_load_start <= 1'b0;
480        1/1                      o_layer_cfg_valid <= 1'b0;
481        1/1                      o_pg_tile_start   <= 1'b0;
482        1/1                      o_fc_tile_start   <= 1'b0;
483                     
484        2/2                      if (i_tile_in_done) tile_in_done_seen <= 1'b1;
                        MISSING_ELSE
485        2/2                      if (i_layer_done)   layer_done_seen <= 1'b1;
                        MISSING_ELSE
486        2/2                      if (i_ld_done)      ld_done_seen <= 1'b1;
                        MISSING_ELSE
487                     
488                                 // ---- Weight load ----
489        1/1                      case (wsvc)
490                                     W_IDLE: begin
491        1/1                              if (chunk_accept) begin
492        1/1                                  svc_kbase <= nx_kbase;
493        1/1                                  svc_len   <= nx_len;
494        1/1                                  buf_half  <= ~buf_half;                 // load into the half not being read
495        1/1                                  wsvc      <= W_ISSUE;
496                                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE
497                                     end
498        2/2                          W_ISSUE: if (i_wload_ready) wsvc <= W_WAIT;
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
499                                     W_WAIT:
500        1/1                          if (i_wload_done) begin
501        1/1                              if (state == C_PE_WAIT) begin           // mid-tile chunk
502        1/1                                  wtag_valid[buf_half] <= 1'b0;       // tag invalid: half overwritten
503        1/1                                  wsvc <= W_REPLY;
504                                         end else begin
505        1/1                                  first_chunk_rdy      <= 1'b1;
506        1/1                                  wtag_valid[buf_half] <= 1'b1;
507        1/1                                  wtag_layer[buf_half] <= layer_idx;
508        1/1                                  wtag_oc[buf_half]    <= oc_group;
509        1/1                                  wsvc                 <= W_IDLE;
510                                         end
511                                     end
                        MISSING_ELSE
512        1/1                          default: wsvc <= W_IDLE;                    // W_REPLY
513                                 endcase
514                     
515                                 // ---- Parameter load ----
516        1/1                      if (pmem_req_fire) begin
517        1/1                          pl_busy   <= 1'b1;
518        1/1                          pl_addr_q <= o_param_mem_addr;
519        1/1                          pl_idx    <= pl_idx + 1'b1;
520                                 end
                        MISSING_ELSE
521        1/1                      if (pmem_rsp_fire) begin
522        1/1                          pl_busy <= 1'b0;
523                                 end
                        MISSING_ELSE
524        2/2                      if (i_param_load_done) pl_done <= 1'b1;
                        MISSING_ELSE
525                     
526                                 // ---- Main FSM ----
527        1/1                      case (state)
528                                     C_IDLE: begin
529        1/1                              o_write_grant <= 1'b0;
530        2/2                              wtag_valid[0] <= 1'b0;  wtag_valid[1] <= 1'b0;
531        1/1                              ld_done_seen  <= 1'b0;
532        1/1                              if (i_start_valid && o_start_ready) begin
533        1/1                                  o_ld_start <= 1'b1;                             // Image RAM -> input_buf
534        1/1                                  state      <= C_START;
535                                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE
536                                     end
537                     
538                                     C_START: begin // image load
539        1/1                              if (i_ld_done || ld_done_seen) begin
540        1/1                                  ld_done_seen        <= 1'b0;
541        1/1                                  o_write_grant       <= 1'b1;
542        1/1                                  layer_idx           <= 3'd0;
543        1/1                                  region_sel          <= 1'b0;
544        1/1                                  pl_idx              <= {(`PARAM_AW+1){1'b0}};
545        1/1                                  pl_busy             <= 1'b0;
546        1/1                                  pl_done             <= 1'b0;
547        1/1                                  o_param_load_start  <= 1'b1;                    // seen on first clk of C_PARAM_LOAD
548        1/1                                  state               <= C_PARAM_LOAD;
549                                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE
550                                     end
551                     
552                                     C_PARAM_LOAD: begin // RAM owner = param
553        2/2                              if (pl_done) state <= C_SET;
                        MISSING_ELSE
554                                     end
555                     
556                                     C_SET: begin // layer descriptor; reset tile counters
557        1/1                              o_layer_cfg_valid <= 1'b1;
558        1/1                              pos_group          <= {`POS_W{1'b0}};
559        1/1                              oc_group           <= {`OCG_W{1'b0}};
560        1/1                              og_off_q           <= {`W_AW{1'b0}};
561        1/1                              tile_started <= 1'b0;
562        1/1                              tcfg_sent <= 1'b0;
563        1/1                              first_chunk_rdy <= 1'b0;
564        1/1                              tile_in_done_seen <= 1'b0;
565        1/1                              layer_done_seen <= 1'b0;
566        1/1                              state <= C_W_LOAD;
567                                     end
568                     
569                                     C_W_LOAD: begin // tile's first weight chunk (skipped on tag hit)
570        1/1                              buf_half <= first_half;
571        1/1                              if (wload_skip) begin
572        1/1                                  first_chunk_rdy <= 1'b1;
573        1/1                              end else if (!first_chunk_rdy && (wsvc == W_IDLE)) begin
574        1/1                                  svc_kbase     <= {`K_W{1'b0}};
575        1/1                                  svc_len       <= first_len;
576        1/1                                  wsvc <= W_ISSUE;
577                                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE
578                     
579        1/1                              if (first_chunk_rdy && (wsvc == W_IDLE)) begin
580        1/1                                  state <= C_TILE_CFG;
581                                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE
582                                     end
583                     
584                                     // Send tile_cfg, then wait for i_params_ready (valid from the clk after cfg).
585                                     C_TILE_CFG: begin
586        1/1                              if (!tcfg_sent) begin
587        2/2                                  if (i_tile_ready) tcfg_sent <= 1'b1;
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
588        1/1                              end else if (i_params_ready) begin
589        1/1                                  tile_started <= 1'b0;
590        1/1                                  state        <= C_PE_TILE;
591                                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE
592                                     end
593                     
594                                     // tile_start to act_path, then tile cmd to pe_cntl
595                                     C_PE_TILE: begin
596        1/1                              if (!tile_started) begin
597        2/2                                  if (ly_is_fc) o_fc_tile_start <= 1'b1;
598        1/1                                  else o_pg_tile_start <= 1'b1;
599        1/1                                  tile_started      <= 1'b1;
600        1/1                                  tile_in_done_seen <= 1'b0;
601        1/1                              end else if (i_tile_ready) begin
602        1/1                                  state <= C_PE_WAIT;
603                                         end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
604                                     end
605                     
606                                     // PE running; mid-tile chunks are served by wsvc above.
607                                     C_PE_WAIT: begin
608        1/1                              if ((i_tile_in_done || tile_in_done_seen) && i_tile_ready)
609        1/1                                  state <= C_NXT_TILE;
                        MISSING_ELSE
610                                     end
611                     
612                                     // oc_group = inner loop, pos_group = outer loop
613                                     C_NXT_TILE: begin
614        1/1                              tile_started      <= 1'b0;
615        1/1                              tcfg_sent         <= 1'b0;
616        1/1                              first_chunk_rdy   <= 1'b0;
617        1/1                              tile_in_done_seen <= 1'b0;
618                     
619        1/1                              if (!oc_last) begin
620        1/1                                  oc_group  <= oc_group + 1'b1;
621        1/1                                  og_off_q  <= og_off_q + k_ext;
622        1/1                                  state <= C_W_LOAD;
623        1/1                              end else if (!pos_last) begin
624        1/1                                  oc_group  <= {`OCG_W{1'b0}};
625        1/1                                  og_off_q  <= {`W_AW{1'b0}};
626        1/1                                  pos_group <= pos_group + 1'b1;
627        1/1                                  state <= C_W_LOAD;
628                                         end else begin
629        1/1                                  state <= C_LAYER_END;
630                                         end
631                                     end
632                     
633                                     // wait for out_path to finish storing the layer
634                                     C_LAYER_END: begin
635        1/1                              if (i_layer_done || layer_done_seen) begin
636        1/1                                  layer_done_seen <= 1'b0;
637        1/1                                  if (layer_idx == (NUM_LAYERS - 1)) begin
638        1/1                                      state <= C_DONE;
639                                             end else begin
640        1/1                                      region_sel <= ~region_sel;
641        1/1                                      layer_idx  <= layer_idx + 1'b1;
642        1/1                                      state      <= C_SET;
643                                             end
644                                         end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
645                                     end
646                     
647                                     default: begin  // C_DONE
648        1/1                              o_write_grant <= 1'b0;
649        1/1                              state         <= C_IDLE;
650                                     end
651                                 endcase
652                             end
653                         end
654                     
655                         // ---- debug ----
656                         // synthesis translate_off
657                         // VCS coverage off
658                         logic [8*12-1:0] state_name;
659                         logic [ 8*8-1:0] wsvc_name;
660                         logic [ 8*5-1:0] layer_name;
661                         logic [8*16-1:0] tile_name;
662                         always_comb begin
663        unreachable          case (layer_idx)
664        unreachable              3'd0:    layer_name = "Conv0";
665        unreachable              3'd1:    layer_name = "Conv1";
666        unreachable              3'd2:    layer_name = "fc1";
667        unreachable              3'd3:    layer_name = "fc2";
668        unreachable              default: layer_name = "L?";
669                             endcase
670        unreachable          $sformat(tile_name, "L%0d p%0d oc%0d", layer_idx, pos_group, oc_group);
671        unreachable          case (state)
672        unreachable              C_IDLE:       state_name = "C_IDLE";
673        unreachable              C_START:      state_name = "C_START";
674        unreachable              C_PARAM_LOAD: state_name = "C_PARAM_LOAD";
675        unreachable              C_SET:        state_name = "C_SET";
676        unreachable              C_W_LOAD:     state_name = "C_W_LOAD";
677        unreachable              C_TILE_CFG:   state_name = "C_TILE_CFG";
678        unreachable              C_PE_TILE:    state_name = "C_PE_TILE";
679        unreachable              C_PE_WAIT:    state_name = "C_PE_WAIT";
680        unreachable              C_NXT_TILE:   state_name = "C_NXT_TILE";
681        unreachable              C_LAYER_END:  state_name = "C_LAYER_END";
682        unreachable              C_DONE:       state_name = "C_DONE";
683        unreachable              default:      state_name = "C_???";
684                             endcase
685        unreachable          case (wsvc)
686        unreachable              W_IDLE:  wsvc_name = "W_IDLE";
687        unreachable              W_ISSUE: wsvc_name = "W_ISSUE";
688        unreachable              W_WAIT:  wsvc_name = "W_WAIT";
689        unreachable              default: wsvc_name = "W_REPLY";

513                    endcase
514        
515                    // ---- Parameter load ----
516                    if (pmem_req_fire) begin
                       -10-  
517                        pl_busy   <= 1'b1;
                           ==>
518                        pl_addr_q <= o_param_mem_addr;
519                        pl_idx    <= pl_idx + 1'b1;
520                    end
                       MISSING_ELSE
                       ==>
521                    if (pmem_rsp_fire) begin
                       -11-  
522                        pl_busy <= 1'b0;
                           ==>
523                    end
                       MISSING_ELSE
                       ==>
524                    if (i_param_load_done) pl_done <= 1'b1;
                       -12-  
                       ==>
                       MISSING_ELSE
                       ==>
525        
526                    // ---- Main FSM ----
527                    case (state)
                       -13-  
528                        C_IDLE: begin
529                            o_write_grant <= 1'b0;
530                            wtag_valid[0] <= 1'b0;  wtag_valid[1] <= 1'b0;
531                            ld_done_seen  <= 1'b0;
532                            if (i_start_valid && o_start_ready) begin
                               -14-  
533                                o_ld_start <= 1'b1;                             // Image RAM -> input_buf
                                   ==>
534                                state      <= C_START;
535                            end
                               MISSING_ELSE
                               ==>
536                        end
537        
538                        C_START: begin // image load
539                            if (i_ld_done || ld_done_seen) begin
                               -15-  
540                                ld_done_seen        <= 1'b0;
                                   ==>
541                                o_write_grant       <= 1'b1;
542                                layer_idx           <= 3'd0;
543                                region_sel          <= 1'b0;
544                                pl_idx              <= {(`PARAM_AW+1){1'b0}};
545                                pl_busy             <= 1'b0;
546                                pl_done             <= 1'b0;
547                                o_param_load_start  <= 1'b1;                    // seen on first clk of C_PARAM_LOAD
548                                state               <= C_PARAM_LOAD;
549                            end
                               MISSING_ELSE
                               ==>
550                        end
551        
552                        C_PARAM_LOAD: begin // RAM owner = param
553                            if (pl_done) state <= C_SET;
                               -16-  
                               ==>
                               MISSING_ELSE
                               ==>
554                        end
555        
556                        C_SET: begin // layer descriptor; reset tile counters
557                            o_layer_cfg_valid <= 1'b1;
                               ==>
558                            pos_group          <= {`POS_W{1'b0}};
559                            oc_group           <= {`OCG_W{1'b0}};
560                            og_off_q           <= {`W_AW{1'b0}};
561                            tile_started <= 1'b0;
562                            tcfg_sent <= 1'b0;
563                            first_chunk_rdy <= 1'b0;
564                            tile_in_done_seen <= 1'b0;
565                            layer_done_seen <= 1'b0;
566                            state <= C_W_LOAD;
567                        end
568        
569                        C_W_LOAD: begin // tile's first weight chunk (skipped on tag hit)
570                            buf_half <= first_half;
571                            if (wload_skip) begin
                               -17-  
572                                first_chunk_rdy <= 1'b1;
                                   ==>
573                            end else if (!first_chunk_rdy && (wsvc == W_IDLE)) begin
                                        -18-  
574                                svc_kbase     <= {`K_W{1'b0}};
                                   ==>
575                                svc_len       <= first_len;
576                                wsvc <= W_ISSUE;
577                            end
                               MISSING_ELSE
                               ==>
578        
579                            if (first_chunk_rdy && (wsvc == W_IDLE)) begin
                               -19-  
580                                state <= C_TILE_CFG;
                                   ==>
581                            end
                               MISSING_ELSE
                               ==>
582                        end
583        
584                        // Send tile_cfg, then wait for i_params_ready (valid from the clk after cfg).
585                        C_TILE_CFG: begin
586                            if (!tcfg_sent) begin
                               -20-  
587                                if (i_tile_ready) tcfg_sent <= 1'b1;
                                   -21-  
                                   ==>
                                   MISSING_ELSE
                                   ==>
588                            end else if (i_params_ready) begin
                                        -22-  
589                                tile_started <= 1'b0;
                                   ==>
590                                state        <= C_PE_TILE;
591                            end
                               MISSING_ELSE
                               ==>
592                        end
593        
594                        // tile_start to act_path, then tile cmd to pe_cntl
595                        C_PE_TILE: begin
596                            if (!tile_started) begin
                               -23-  
597                                if (ly_is_fc) o_fc_tile_start <= 1'b1;
                                   -24-  
                                   ==>
598                                else o_pg_tile_start <= 1'b1;
                                   ==>
599                                tile_started      <= 1'b1;
600                                tile_in_done_seen <= 1'b0;
601                            end else if (i_tile_ready) begin
                                        -25-  
602                                state <= C_PE_WAIT;
                                   ==>
603                            end
                               MISSING_ELSE
                               ==>
604                        end
605        
606                        // PE running; mid-tile chunks are served by wsvc above.
607                        C_PE_WAIT: begin
608                            if ((i_tile_in_done || tile_in_done_seen) && i_tile_ready)
                               -26-  
609                                state <= C_NXT_TILE;
                                   ==>
                                   MISSING_ELSE
                                   ==>
610                        end
611        
612                        // oc_group = inner loop, pos_group = outer loop
613                        C_NXT_TILE: begin
614                            tile_started      <= 1'b0;
615                            tcfg_sent         <= 1'b0;
616                            first_chunk_rdy   <= 1'b0;
617                            tile_in_done_seen <= 1'b0;
618        
619                            if (!oc_last) begin
                               -27-  
620                                oc_group  <= oc_group + 1'b1;
                                   ==>
621                                og_off_q  <= og_off_q + k_ext;
622                                state <= C_W_LOAD;
623                            end else if (!pos_last) begin
                                        -28-  
624                                oc_group  <= {`OCG_W{1'b0}};
                                   ==>
625                                og_off_q  <= {`W_AW{1'b0}};
626                                pos_group <= pos_group + 1'b1;
627                                state <= C_W_LOAD;
628                            end else begin
629                                state <= C_LAYER_END;
                                   ==>
630                            end
631                        end
632        
633                        // wait for out_path to finish storing the layer
634                        C_LAYER_END: begin
635                            if (i_layer_done || layer_done_seen) begin
                               -29-  
636                                layer_done_seen <= 1'b0;
637                                if (layer_idx == (NUM_LAYERS - 1)) begin
                                   -30-  
638                                    state <= C_DONE;
                                       ==>
639                                end else begin
640                                    region_sel <= ~region_sel;
                                       ==>
641                                    layer_idx  <= layer_idx + 1'b1;
642                                    state      <= C_SET;
643                                end
644                            end
                               MISSING_ELSE
                               ==>
645                        end
646        
647                        default: begin  // C_DONE
648                            o_write_grant <= 1'b0;
                               ==>
